# Review — 048-govern-acquired-runtime

## Summary

Five passes over 048's window since its last review (diff-base 0af1f01a, base-source last-review): the 60 window commits that are 048's, whose subject names 048 or no spec, of 987 in the window. Six reviewers: four by behavior (the pointer and the store, tasks 29 and 31; task 30's bounded probes and startup environment reads; the removal of Auggie, Antigravity and OpenCode; the unnamed chores and governance commits, with 048's own artifacts), and two rule by rule over all 310 loaded rules (125 and 185), each rule file read whole. examined counts the 98 in-scope files a pass read: each by the hunks of 048's commits plus named context ranges, the large runtime files (schema/primitives.rs, primitives/mod.rs, main.rs, mcp/server.rs, check_artifacts.rs, fetch_archive.rs, dashboard.rs, tests/mcp.rs) by those hunks only, runtime/CHANGELOG.md and runtime/Cargo.lock by their hunks, and the 8 chore files the handoff named in depth. Not counted: framework/bootstrap/govern.md, byte-identical to ductus.md, which a pass read, by cmp and scripts/audit/transitional-bootstrap-parity.sh; scripts/mutation/destination-checks.py, whose hunks were judged by python3 scripts/mutation/run.py --check (0 invalid anchors), not read; and specs/040-configurable-specs-dir/spec.md, which only fix round 1 changed and that round's re-read read. The passes found one high-confidence MUST, BE-TIMEOUT-001 on the shipped hook's unbounded runtime calls, waived with the owner's reason; four low-confidence findings, which block nothing; and three broken contracts (AC3's halt named no digest, the seed claims 022 task 18 made false, AC24's sweep missing spec 040), fixed in fix round 1 with thirteen chores and the hook's bare watchdog literals (CFG-CONST-003). Fix round 1's re-read found one SHOULD, the data model's seed sentence, fixed by its prescribed edits in round 2, which the diff check matched. Two Windows cases no run has reached are deferred, by the owner, to tasks.md task 32. The owner's two task 30 waivers are recorded, twenty observations are waived by the rule with their reasons, and three by their stored decisions. The fetch-archive DNS finding was added at the last write, after the hook waiver was stored, because a new waiver's files include every file its rule fires at.

2 fix round(s): 1 re-read, 1 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

## MUST violations (blocking)

*None.*

## SHOULD violations (advisory)

*None.*

## Low-confidence findings

### LOW-CONFIDENCE: BE-TIMEOUT-001 — fetch-archive's host lookups use the system resolver's unnamed default bound, outside FETCH_TIMEOUT and FETCH_DEADLINE

- **File**: `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs:386-396`
- **Rule**: Every outbound network call, downstream-service call, or blocking wait on another process or host MUST be made under a bounded timeout — no call waits unboundedly for a response.
- **Finding**: Screen::system resolves through std::net's to_socket_addrs, and validate_fetch_url screens the first host before the client and its timeouts exist, so the lookup waits on the system resolver's own default, which no constant names. The resolver does bound it, which is why this is low confidence.
- **Auto-fixable**: no

### LOW-CONFIDENCE: BE-RACE-001 — two concurrent /ductus runs share the store's one fixed tempfile name

- **File**: `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md:427`
- **Rule**: Shared mutable state reachable from more than one concurrent execution context MUST be protected by a synchronization mechanism — a lock, an atomic primitive, single-owner/actor confinement, or serialized access.
- **Finding**: Branch 2 step 6 writes the store through `{store-path}.tmp`, a name every run shares, so a second run's cp could truncate the file after the first run's mv put it in place; spec 048's Edge Cases say a reader sees never a partial file. Unmeasured: it rests on cp and mv semantics.
- **Auto-fixable**: no

### LOW-CONFIDENCE: BE-PAYLOAD-004 — fetch-archive buffers a download of up to 256 MiB whole in memory

- **File**: `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs:1086-1104`
- **Rule**: Request or response bodies that can be large SHOULD be streamed in bounded chunks rather than buffered whole in memory.
- **Finding**: read_capped holds the body whole, up to a named 256 MiB cap, so the digest is verified before anything is written; whether that is large relative to the process's memory is unconfirmed.
- **Auto-fixable**: no

### LOW-CONFIDENCE: QUAL-GROUND-001 — the proxy and certificate capture restate how hyper-util and rustls read their environment, with no test against the libraries

- **File**: `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs:627-733`
- **Rule**: Code whose correctness depends on a contract defined outside that code — a database schema, another service's API shape, a file or wire format, or a list, schema, or format defined elsewhere in the repository — SHOULD bind to that contract so that a wrong assumption fails loudly: a typed or generated binding, a schema or migration reference the build or a test checks, a startup or first-use validation, or a test that exercises the real shape.
- **Finding**: ProxyEnv and CertEnv encode which variables hyper-util's matcher and rustls-native-certs read, citing the locked versions; no test compares the capture with the libraries' own reads, so a library change would go unnoticed.
- **Auto-fixable**: no

## Waived findings

### WAIVED: BE-TIMEOUT-001 — the hook's derive-dependencies, derive-references, check-corpus-links and check-artifact-size calls wait on the runtime with no bound

- **File**: `framework/bootstrap/hooks/ductus-pre-commit:200-285`
- **Rule**: Every outbound network call, downstream-service call, or blocking wait on another process or host MUST be made under a bounded timeout — no call waits unboundedly for a response.
- **Finding**: Lines 200, 201, 280 and 285 call the runtime with no timeout; only the guard's four probes run under the watchdog. A runtime that hangs in one of these calls hangs the commit.
- **Auto-fixable**: no
- **Waived**: Owner, 2026-11-12 (048 tasks.md task 30) and 2026-11-13 (this review): the hook's derive-dependencies, derive-references, check-corpus-links and check-artifact-size calls stay unbounded, since a fixed bound could kill a large repository's derive, which is slow and not hung; the guard's four probes are bounded by its watchdog.

## Deferred findings

*None.*

- security — DUCTUS_FETCH_ALLOW_INSECURE_HOSTS lets an operator reach a named host over plain http — `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs` — **waived**: An opt-in escape hatch an operator sets, documented in docs/runtime.md and 022's scenarios, that predates this window. (decided by the rule)
- bug — the bootstrap's checksum-mismatch halt named neither the expected nor the computed digest, which AC3 requires — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- bug — 048's data model, spec and plan and the release workflow said curl and tar are seeded and named four agent seeds, which 022 task 18 and the Auggie, Antigravity and OpenCode removal made false — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/data-model.md` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- bug — AC24's sweep left spec 040 calling the runtime optional — `specs/040-configurable-specs-dir/spec.md` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed** (decided by the rule)

- convention — the pointer's prose lagged AC13: the data model and spec said it resolves to the binary, the state-a scenario called the store current, and the bootstrap cited an ambiguous step 5 — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/data-model.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — the hook's comment and the state-a scenario counted two --help and two --version probes where the guard runs four --help probes and at most one --version echo — `framework/bootstrap/hooks/ductus-pre-commit` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — the hook's watchdog used bare 10-second and 1-second sleeps where /ductus names {probe-timeout} — `framework/bootstrap/hooks/ductus-pre-commit` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — the bootstrap's framework archive fetch was the one curl without --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 and time bounds — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — the measurement harness lower-cases certutil's digest, and the procedure did not say its digest comparisons ignore case — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — fetch_archive.rs cited hyper-util 0.1.20 and rustls-platform-verifier 0.7.0 where the lockfile holds 0.1.21 and 0.7.1 — `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — fetch-archive-startup-env.py said deleting the certificate capture changes no observable behavior, which fetch_archive_cert_env_startup.rs observes — `scripts/mutation/fetch-archive-startup-env.py` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — a bare block was left in host_config.rs's a_block_refuses_the_rows_that_name_no_command after its loop shrank to one entry — `runtime/src/primitives/host_config.rs` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- bug — the proxy-once scenario's Edge Case 4 said an uncaptured process reads nothing from the environment, though it reads SSL_CERT_FILE and SSL_CERT_DIR — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/scenarios/fetch-archive-reads-its-proxy-once.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — the retired-namespace scenario's State A bullet described a shape no remaining agent reaches — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/scenarios/retired-namespace-tools-are-off-limits.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — tasks.md task 28's owed list and task 30's land-048-30 sentence were stale — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/tasks.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — plan.md's Affected Files omitted the hook, primitives/mod.rs, the two startup-env tests, the measurement script and the temp-env dev-dependency — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/plan.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — Windows unelevated without Developer Mode is unmeasured, though AC13 claims the pointer needs no elevated privileges — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/scenarios/pointer-follows-the-store.md` — **deferred** to `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/tasks.md` task 32
- other — no Windows run has had a host write State B's --*-json files into {tempdir}, which the CLI must find inside a staging directory — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` — **deferred** to `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/tasks.md` task 32
- other — a non-UTF-8 proxy variable reads as unset, so that scheme's fetches go direct — `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs` — **waived**: Owner, 2026-11-12 (048 tasks.md task 30): contrived, the fix reaching every proxy read.
- convention — the bootstrap interpolates {tempdir} unquoted, so a TMPDIR holding whitespace splits the operands — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` — **waived**: Owner, 2026-11-12 (048 tasks.md task 30): the plan states that a TMPDIR holding whitespace is unsupported.
- bug — a hung probe or a failed mktemp halts the hook with 'It has no <primitive> subcommand', naming the wrong cause — `framework/bootstrap/hooks/ductus-pre-commit` — **waived**: By design: the hook reads a probe its watchdog ended as a failed probe, as its comment says, and halts either way naming the binary and its --version; a message per cause is a new branch and test, not a mechanical fix. (decided by the rule)
- other — State A's pointer check adds a restart notice for any re-make, a missing or dangling pointer included — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` — **waived**: The extra restart is the conservative direction and never leaves a stale server running; telling the cases apart would rest on how the live server was spawned, which the procedure cannot see. (decided by the rule)
- other — nothing fails if the pointer procedure regresses: its one automated artifact is the measurement script, which never fails on an outcome — `scripts/measure-replace-under-server.sh` — **waived**: Pointer materialization is host-executed procedure prose, which no unit test can run; its composition test is the real adopter bootstrap the release program owes, and runtime.yml's measurement for Windows. (decided by the rule)
- convention — the measurement harness passes certutil a cygpath -w path where the procedure passes the Git Bash path — `scripts/measure-replace-under-server.sh` — **waived**: Git Bash converts a POSIX path argument for a native program itself, so cygpath -w is the conversion the procedure's spelling already gets; the case difference is fixed in the procedure. (decided by the rule)
- other — the pointer's recorded rationale lost the Claude ${VAR} alternative when the Auggie, Antigravity and OpenCode sweep deleted its Resolved Question — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/spec.md` — **waived**: The pointer stays the one path Claude's .mcp.json, Pi's bridge, the hook and State B's CLI calls all name; whether Claude's ${VAR} expansion should replace it there is a design change outside this review. (decided by the rule)
- convention — HostRow::CommandOrSkill and the Command/skill path row label still name skills no remaining agent has — `runtime/src/primitives/host_config.rs` — **waived**: Naming only: the variant carries both remaining agents' command paths, and a rename touches the runtime and its tests for no behavior. (decided by the rule)
- other — no end-to-end test resolves a claude-style commands/{project} command under a non-default host block since the exec fixtures became Pi-shaped — `runtime/tests/exec_subprocess.rs` — **waived**: host_config.rs's unit tests hold the candidate order that end-to-end case relied on. (decided by the rule)
- other — Family 2, manifest-parity.sh, is now a single-file check — `scripts/audit/manifest-parity.sh` — **waived**: It says so in its header and in its stderr count, so it reads as narrowed, never as a clean check of more. (decided by the rule)
- other — three migration procedures and runtime-store-path's target_paths were edited in place rather than by an ordered new entry — `framework/migrations.toml` — **waived**: The edits removed only the dropped agents' paths, which no supported adopter has, so nothing an adopter who already ran them needs is lost. (decided by the rule)
- other — the Auggie, Antigravity and OpenCode removal has had no real adopter or pi run — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` — **waived**: 012's task 12, its real-adopter bootstrap, is an unchecked box that tracks it and holds 012 short of done. (decided by the rule)
- other — the constitution is 49,969 bytes of its 50,000-byte ceiling — `framework/constitution.md` — **waived**: It is within the ceiling scripts/lint-governing-size.sh holds, and the next addition retires a rule instead, as spec 072 intends. (decided by the rule)
- convention — DUCTUS_FETCH_ALLOW_INSECURE_HOSTS reads a non-UTF-8 value as an empty allowlist — `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs` — **waived**: It fails closed, exempting no host, the class of the proxy read the owner waived on 2026-11-12. (decided by the rule)
- convention — the release workflow's retry honors Retry-After only as seconds and keeps every redirect hop's headers — `.github/workflows/runtime-release.yml` — **waived**: BE-RETRY-003's hint is advisory and the comment states the seconds-only form; tail reads the last header written, the final hop's when it carries one. (decided by the rule)
- other — Screen::new's Proxy::all filter changes no fetch's outcome, since run refuses a fetch whose proxy parse failed — `runtime/src/primitives/fetch_archive.rs` — **waived**: A unit test, only_named_proxies_reqwest_accepts_are_exempt, builds a Screen from a rejected proxy and pins the filter, which keeps the screen right whatever order run checks in. (decided by the rule)
- other — CFG-ENV-008 and CFG-ENV-001 are met by owner decisions recorded in the proxy-once scenario, not by the code's shape — `specs/048-govern-acquired-runtime/scenarios/fetch-archive-reads-its-proxy-once.md` — **waived**: Those decisions are the scenario's Resolved Questions, which the code implements; nothing is outstanding. (decided by the rule)
- other — whether Pi's shell tool applies the {probe-timeout} the bootstrap sets on every --version probe is unverified — `framework/bootstrap/ductus.md` — **waived**: The bootstrap names the bound and how a host applies it, as BE-TIMEOUT-001 asks of a design; how one host's shell tool honors a timeout is that host's behavior, which the bootstrap cannot change. (decided by the rule)
- other — a new --waive's files include those of low-confidence findings of its rule, which need no waiver, so this run's first write held back the fetch-archive DNS finding to keep the owner's hook waiver to the hook — `runtime/src/primitives/write_review.rs` — **waived**: Pipeline machinery outside 048, observed while reviewing framework work; this record is right, since its hook waiver was stored by a write holding no other BE-TIMEOUT-001 finding and the final write passes it as applied. (decided by the rule)
- reuse: runtime_binary, ensure_binary_built and the envelope loop are copied across test files — `runtime/tests/exec_subprocess.rs:18` — **waived**: predates the window; consolidating test helpers is a refactor outside 048
- errors: InvalidSlug and InvalidPath print a rejected value raw, a newline included — `runtime/src/primitives/mod.rs:332` — **waived**: the value reaches callers inside a JSON error envelope, which escapes it
- robustness: check-release-tag.sh strips all whitespace from `version`, so a two-line file collapses — `scripts/check-release-tag.sh` — **waived**: the collapsed value refuses every real tag, and Family 20 names the extra line in the same audit job publish needs, so nothing publishes and the cause is reported
